> **dude1286**: The 3 Heart Challenge has been around for a while in the Zelda community. It can be done on Zelda 1 if certain bosses are defeated in a certain way. It makes sense to add the challenge as an achievement to the games it is available in.

> **russolini77**: Whether the challenge has been around for a while or not, achievements that force the player to complete the game at a disadvantage are unnecessary and beyond the scope of a 100% completion.  Achievements should be fun, not hair-pullingly difficult. 

> **dude1286**: If you want hair pulling difficulty in OoT, try the Uber Challenge. The 3 Heart Challenge is not difficult if the player knows the game mechanics. The only enemies that should give players issues during the run would be Stalfos and Iron Knuckles.

## Achievements

### Spider Net (10 Points | 14 True Ratio)
**Type:** missable

**Description:** Defeat Queen Gohma before she can retreat to the ceiling

![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158117.png)

#### Tricks & Gotchas
> **Lancerlott**: Simple enough. Start by Z-targetting and shooting her in the eye. Jump-Slash with a Deku Stick while still targetted, and after the slash connects, drop Z-Target (hit button again) and Crouch-Stab until dead. 

> **Lancerlott**: Slingshot stuns longer than Deku Nut.

> **p4**: Is using a glitch the only way of doing this?

> **bzeth**: En espaol: Empezar con la mira Z, disparar al ojo.Ataque en salto usando la vara deku mientras sigue targeteada, despues de esto quitar la mira z (presionando el boton de nuevo) y apualar agachado (es decir te cubres y atacas a la vez)

> **warski**: this was tough but I did it. So this is how it goes - lock-on - use sling shot - switch to stick - jump stick attack - unlock - hold R and stab. The R + sword attack is a low attack that uses the previous attacks damage and the jump stick does 4 damage - so the R + attack is 4 damage per stab.

> **AssClownKing**: Slingshot stun, then 5 deku sticks. So it was close as possible and hope for the best. Took me two tries, no glitching necessary.

> **HylianBrat**: I was having some trouble with this at first but the tip to switch the z-target from toggle to hold in the options that @CatKingKeefie mentioned really made a difference.  Got it first try after that.

> **victoryrhoads**: In main menu screen, switch settings to lock "Hold"
> (Lock onto Gohma and don't let go of the trigger)
> Stun Gohma with the Slingshot and do the following quickly after:
> After the stun, your next movement should be pulling out the Deku Stick.
> Keeping in mind, you've never stopped being locked onto Gohma, perform the jump attack while holding the Deku Stick.
> NOW release the lock-on, hit & hold R to crouch and then just unleash your crouch-stab to finish him off.
> To perform crouch-stab, you're just holding R (NOT locked on) and hitting the attack button. Don't hold forward or anything else; just hold R and hit attack. Practice on enemies at the beginning of the stage if you need to get the flow down.

> **IamVitinho**: After at least 30 tries, I took a break, basically giving up. I was sure this achievement was impossible... Tried one last time after the break and got it on the first try lol.
> 
> Switching Z-Targeting to Hold helped a lot (thanks @CatKingKeefie).
> 
> Enter the room, no need to move, just look up so Gohma notices you. Use the Slingshot, switch to a Deku Stick, then do the Jump Attack with the stick. You’ll land in the perfect spot to stab her eye (Shield + Attack).
> 
> I think it’s important to hit her in the eye. I tried many times doing all the steps correctly, and she still ran away.
> 
> Thank you guys!

> **CherriBomb**: I can't believe how difficult some people here made this out to be. I did it on my first try, just shot Gohma in the eye with Slingshot, then hit her with 5 Deku Sticks, and she was done.

> **emberlmao**: CherriBomb, thank you. I was struggling so hard trying to carry out these previous overcomplicated methods. Slightshot + 5 Deku sticks (NO JUMP SLASH NEEDED) works wonders. Anything else is such a waste of time.

> **kennycastles**: Can vouch for the 5 deku sticks method, it's not difficult at all this way

> **Bamamchi**: This took me an embarrasing amount of tries

> **Daniel04k**: One thing I've noticed people don't pay much attention to, especially me, is that after hitting her in the eye, you should quickly move into the attack by jumping, closing in on her, and continuing to hit her. But the important thing is to do it very quickly. Salve galera do Brasil e dos USA

> **MarioRPG**: Easy.

> **PandoraMegistus**: Finally got it! Definitely don't need the harder methods, one hit with the slingshot and the five Deku Sticks worked wonders. You just have to be fast!

> **CNPurple**: Queen Gohma, Slingshot, Stick Strike, Crouch Stab.
> 
> https://www.twitch.tv/videos/2833435435

> **GNEiL48**: What @CNPurple posted works.
> 
> Paused when I entered the room before triggering the boss fight. 
> 
> Came to the comments to see what people had done, saw that comment. 
> 
> Triggered the fight, locked on, sling shot, stick jump attack, crouch stab, boss dead. 
> 
> EZPZ first try. 
> 
> Played so many times I was going to use the stick anyways.
> 
> The reason this works is because the Deku Stick jump attack does 4 damage, and the crouch stab scales to the last attack you did. 
> 
> So you can jump slash with the deku stick and store 4 damage stab attacks. 
> 
> The damage is reset when entering a different room though, so you can’t stick jump attack and then go in the boss fight expecting to Sling shot and stab twice.
> 
> For future knowledge the Master Sword jump attack also does 4 damage, and the Biggoron sword jump attack does 8 DAMAGE!
> 
> If you do a normal sword attack during the fight the stab is set to 2 damage, and if you haven’t attacked at all the base for a stab attack is 1 damage.
> 
> So, as a kid, stick jump attack, stab away. 
> 
> As an adult, jump attack for 4 damage stabs with master sword, jump attack with the biggoron sword, and stab with it, or switch back to the master sword because it will still have the 8 damage stored for big stab damage.

> **CrackieChan94**: Not too hard, don't overthink it, just use 3 sticks but act as quickly as possible. 
> 1- jump attack 
> 2- normal hit
> 3- jump attack 
> That's all you need.

> **itsAshermon**: Ohmygod you guys... this boss is not too hard 
> Step 1: slingshot gohma's eye 
> Step 2: jump slash with a deku stick 
> Step 3: quickly crouch stab twice with a kokiri sword

> **VegasTyke**: https://youtu.be/ZP76oMbVq4Q?is=_ZY13aoQPFsLTrf4
> 
> Slingshot, spam Deku Stick button.

> **EleMentalPhoenix**: I had no problems with this, I set up a Dekustick Jumpslash before entering the fight.
> Then Dekunut, get close, Nut again, and crouchstab 3 times.

> **kaizard04**: não sei como isso me levou algum tempo pra conseguir, usei o metodo de usar o estilingue depois o stick srike e depois só finalizar com o crouch stab, não parece dificil na prática, mas vai render umas boas tentativas com crtz

### Family Reunion (25 Points | 50 True Ratio)
**Description:** Collect 100 Gold Skulltulas and obtain your final reward

![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158199.png)

#### Tricks & Gotchas
> **Server**: BenGhazi edited this achievement.

> **Server**: DrPixel edited this achievement.

> **Server**: SporyTike edited this achievement.

> **Server**: DanielARP edited this achievement.

> **NewWizKid**: This was harder than I thought it would be.

> **Chawk**: There's a skulltula symbol next to the area name on the map when you've collected everything in that area.

> **setebot**: @Chawk DUDE, thank you. I have 98 currently and was about to search the ENTIRE world before you mentioned it. lol

> **BodeX3**: BRO I MADE THIS WAY FING HARDER THAN IT NEEDED TO BE. I decided to bite the bullet THREE TIMES and follow a written guide to find them all but i ended up missing two. The problem is that the emulator crashes randomly and makes it so you loose the token if you didn't save. To make your life easier to find these, heres some tips:
>  -always save after entering a dungeon or getting a token (Duh)
>  -use a video walkthrough, not a written one
>  -like @Chawk said, you can look at the map and it will tell you if you got all the tokens for that area. I thought he meant only for the dungeons... But if you're outside of any dungeon, look at the map and you can see at the bottom where the name of the area. If the skulltula symbol is to the left, then you got them all. If you're in a dungeon, the symbol will be on the top. 

> **acheevmint**: I tried so much to do this without a guide, but one of these in particular involves a bit of moon logic and I'm pretty sure I would have never figured it out on my own. Also, not sure what Nintendo were thinking by giving you practically nothing for 100 gold skulltulas, how about a machine gun? Or a pogo stick?

> **BladeofLeaves**: Not me hitting 99 and running around the entire map trying to find the last one (including learning the reverse path of Haunted Wasteland) just for it to be the one on the wall in the Graveyard

> **Server**: Altomar edited this achievement's logic.

> **LyndisLippi**: It's been said before, but if you have trouble figuring out which skulltulas you are missing (without writing it down beforehand, obviously), check out the map! The world map shows the many zones of the game. But also, it shows if you have collected all the Skulltulas in a zone. Adding a golden Skulltula next to the name of the place. On the world map, it will only show the symbol if all the skulltulas of all the “small zones” that compose the “Big Zone” itself are removed and obtained. Example: Death Mountain ( Map Zone) counts Death Mountain trail, Goron City, etc. But it does NOT count the dungeons. Those have their own mark, on their own map inside the dungeon. It's a bit confusing, but here is a small visual example: (https://imgur.com/gallery/marcas-skulltulas-zelda-oot-bp1BGkZ)

> **CasperRaine**: Can't believe, after all these years, the prize for getting them all is a measly 200 Rupees. 
> 
> Iunno what else the game could give you at that point (Because you have to go through all the dungeons except Ganons Castle to find them all), but man I wish they could've done something creative for it, because chances are, your wallet is probably full anyway like mine was.

> **jamboree**: @CasperRaine it's more than 200 rupees though, because every time you go to him, he'll give you another 200, so effectively the prize is infinite money. That said, yeah, it's totally useless as you're probably way maxed out on rupees anyway.

> **jamboree**: just done this and if you'd asked me as a child, this is one of my proudest gaming achievements - I didn't use a guide! I kind of assumed there were only like 50-60 in the game

> **Meikoix**: I can't believe I'm hitting 99 and the main map, along with the dungeons and temples are marked, I'm losing my sanity

> **Server**: Altomar edited this achievement's description.

> **TheLichKing**: FUTURE TIP: 
> 
> This is where my last one was and it was driving me crazy. 99/100 with Zora's Domain being the only one not completed. As adult Link, you need the Silver Gauntlets. Head over to Zora's Fountain and over to where the great fairy cave is (go at night, just to be safe). There will be a grey boulder that you can pick up due to the gauntlets. Toss it, bomb the rock beneath, and drop down. There will be several enemies hidden (use the Eye) and head on by them to a ladder. The skulltula will be at the top of the area along the left wall.

> **xvvjxr**: Took me an hour, great challenge

> **sandroRetro**: Wow I easily beat the game like 30 times in my life and never realized how hard this fight can be if you do not want to get hit.
> 
> It was a really welcome challenge.

> **Zarbees**: probably the most difficult one so far out of the set

> **Kernumius**: This acheivement is really making me hate the camera to a whole new level.

> **MasterSwordUltima**: For anyone having a rough time with this - forget Din's Fire, deku nuts will take out the jellyfish instantly if they're not tethered to the main body.  So much quicker!

> **assdeper**: How do you give it damage as it spins jellyfishes just after you give ther firsts sword hits?

> **assdeper**: I can't givi it damage at this point https://youtu.be/jV9057TMKvo?si=8Ljlu1kccBoEXJvz&t=47

> **sandroRetro**: either throw the bumerang many times or be very fast there is a small window to stun it right a way with it when the phase starts.

> **assdeper**: Thank you sandro4912 ^_^

> **LovelySoftSnow**: This should not be as hard as it is. Sometimes the jellyfish just fly into you. Sometimes you'll get zapped with no warning on a phase change. You have to get used to when you can and cant Z target something. This is a nightmare

> **LovelySoftSnow**: Got it though because complaining works

> **Ric3737**: Ok, this achievement is aweful, the camera and z-targeting is too inconsistent. I finally got it.
> 
> Phase 1. Move in on Barinade and Z-lock on his tentacles on the ceiling. Throw your boomerang and move sideways. When you have your boomerang back, Z-lock on the next. After hitting the 3rd tentacle, move to the wall as quickly as possible.
> 
> Phase 2. The rotating Bari (jellyfish things) can't hit you if you are against the wall. Z-lock on Barinade and look for an opening while the Bari are rotating. Move in and hit him with a boomerang. Then hit him 5 times with your sword and immediately dodge back.
> 
> Phase 3. This can get tricky because Barinade can chase you down and will hit you against the wall. Try to get a z-lock on as soon as possible and keep moving sideways and back while continuously throwing your boomerang. Once he is stunned, try to get a Z-lock on on one of the Bari and hit it with a boomerang. If you are lucky with the Z-lock on, you may get 2 or 3 in a single stun. Don't rush it though and watch out for the electricity beams. It's better to stay safe and do 1 at a time. Once you are ready to hit the last Bari, try to get hit it while keeping a bigger distance from Barinade.
> 
> Phase 4. If you are too close to Barinade, he will immediatly zap you with lighting, so get some distance and then try to get the Z-lock on on Barinade. Keep moving sideways around him while trying to time a boomerang throw to hit him. Once hit, move in immediately and start slashing with your sword until he burries into the ground. Keep moving sideways to avoid the electricity. Keep an eye on the tentacles that shoot them. Once he gets out of the ground and starts moving again, repeat this step 2 more times and the boss will be dead.

> **10000Noodles**: Using a PS3 controller. This is not great. I can’t even sense myself making progress. Z-targeting and crouchstab seem to only work on weekdays. I don’t get it.

> **10000Noodles**: Finally!

> **SoftPaw6234**: This is a level of pain I haven't felt since I played OOT S-Edition; I almost gave up on hardcore because of this

> **sandroRetro**: I agree even for someone who beat the game easily 30 times this was kinda hell. For sure the hardest cheevo in the set for me.

> **Suvo**: This was an awesome challenge, probably took me 10 attempts. I used the farores wind in the room before the boss to quickly get back there each attempt.

> **Jaace**: Whew, that took a few tries but I finally got it!

> **PrimalLust**: Try try again!!!! Use farores wind to save time. And follow ric3737 advice!!!!

> **Kyzirado**: 1st try! WOOHOO! =]

> **rafaspeedruns**: Let's gooo finally!

> **sheko77**: Después de 8 intentos lo logré!

> **CasperRaine**: Set Farores wind so you can return directly to the room before the boss if you fail and have to reset (Still have to hit the boomerang switch at the beginning, but not having to walk to the room is nice. Also, this achievement is how I found out that you can even get Farores Wind as Child Link xD )
> 
> 4th try. Getting the tentacles off the roof is easy enough. Start of second phase, plant your back against the wall like someone else said, the swinging jellies can't hit you.
> 
> Now, instead of boomeranging each individual jelly, use Deku Nuts (I'm sure someone else mentioned them) You can get two at a time this way, maybe even 3 of you're lucky.
> 
> Phase 3, spam the boomerang at him when he's defending himself, one throw might get through and make it shorter. Spam Deku Nuts at the jellies again until they are all gone. I like to lock on to Barinade and continuously sidestep to avoid his lightning beams.
> 
> Phase 4 is straight forward. Sidestep his beams as he shoots, and try not to get trapped against the wall, and only throw the boomerang if you're confident you'll hit him.
> 
> Some have said crouch stab to damage, but I get nervous so I instead substituted in Deku Stick Jump Attacks for the damage aspect at the end. It takes 3 I believe.
> 
> Good Luck everyone!

> **jamboree**: The worst bit is having to stay to the side during phase 2 and NOT getting hit by his counter attack at the end. Phase 3 and 4 are actually pretty straightforward, if nerve biting. Follow the advice in this thread (a weirdly complicated fight!) and it is easy. I recommend seeing the whole battle through a few times so you get the hang of it, rather than restart as soon as you fail.

> **Daniel04k**: Essa foi difícil, mas eu consegui. Vou platinar esse jogo!!!!

> **GNEiL48**: Hated this one, many restarts because I would get hit by the Jelly fish as soon as a phase would start.
> 
> Be careful with your Z targeting, try to get the timing right to move to the next target once you hit one. 
> 
> First phase I locked on and kept moving to the side not to get hit my lightening, hit the 3 things up top. Once the 3rd is gone I tried to make sure I was facing the boss, and ready to lock on to its main body to hit it with the boomerang to stun it. 
> 
> The part I kept failing at was here, if I was too close I’d get his by the jellyfish when they started spinning because I was either too close, or my camera would mess up, I’d try to get some distance to fix my camera and get hit further out. There was no rhyme or reason to that part, just bad luck and timing I guess. 
> 
> But next I would use the sticks for a long range jump attack, I think I was getting 3-4 off, with each hit breaking a stick, and each hit doing enough damage to remove a jelly fish. 
> 
> After each time the boss would get more intense, but I just tried to watch the blue color fade to be ready to stun instantly again.
> 
> It gets more aggro, but honestly I had it in its most aggro phase and had to take a sec to reset myself because it got really close to a wall, and I didn’t have room to keep moving to the right, so I backed up and got distance. I was afraid if I started immediately moving to the left I would walk back into an electric attack since that was its pattern. 
> 
> The distance gave me the room to start closing in while moving to whichever side I needed, hit the boomerang stun, and another 1-2 stick jump attacks to finish it off.
> 
> Worst achievement so far, but once getting past phase 2 my method worked fine. 
> 
> Not sure if the sword also take a jelly away each attack, but I know the stick jump attacks do more damage from the last two boss achievements, so this was what I used. 
> 
> Plus the longer range made me not have to worry about accidentally walking into the boss body. 
> 
> Hope this helps!

> **Ladybunne**: I made a checklist for this achievement. It's here: https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1CpNkz7kQdhpM8I1OKLiiOJE1PxfbuwKnaove9kdNjtI/edit?usp=sharing
> 
> Here's the path I took:
> 
> - Go to Deku Tree for [#1 Deku Tree's Left Stone] and [#2 Deku Tree's Right Stone].
> - Go to Lost Woods and get [#3 Kokiri Clifftop Stone] just outside.
> - Go left in Lost Woods for [#4 Stick Scrub's Stone].
> - Play Minuet for [#5 Saria's Stone], [#6 Above Maze Right Stone] and [#7 Above Maze Left Stone].
> - Play Prelude, then get [#8 Blinky Stone], [#9 Pinky Stone], [#10 Inky Stone] and [#11 Clyde Stone].
> - Go to Hyrule Castle for [#12 Stone Above The Vines] and [#13 Stone Above The Stone Wall].
> - Play Storms at the tree near the moat for [#14 Stone Beneath The Royal Tree].
> - Play Nocturne for [#15 Non-Grave Stone].
> - Go to Dodongo's Cavern for [#16 Delicious Stone] at the back right of the main room.
> - Go to Goron City for [#17 Unbreakable Stone] and [#18 Medigoron's Stone].
> - Climb Death Mountain Trail for [#19 Biggoron's Stone].
> - Enter Death Mountain Crater and go right for [#20 Antisocial Stone].
> - Play Serenade, then head southeast for [#21 Southeast Drowned Stone].
> - Go clockwise for [#22 Southwest Drowned Stone] and [#23 Little Bridge Stone].
> - Take the Zora's Domain warp for [#24 Mweep Stone].
> - Go to Zora's Fountain for [#25 Jabu's Stone] and [#26 Farore's Stone].
> - Go down to Zora's River for [#27 Sleepless Waterfall Stone].
> - Grab the bottom Cucco and use it to get to the upper grottos area (follow the hidden left river path near the fork).
> - Enter the generic grotto to the left to get [#28 Grotto Stone], then exit and get [#29 River Grottos Stone].
> - Go to Hyrule Field, then bomb the stone circle just outside the Gerudo Valley entrance for [#30 Webbing Stone].
> - Jump down to the ledge at the bottom of Gerudo Valley for [#31 Valley Waterfall Stone].
> - Play Requiem, then walk south for [#32 Sandstone]. The achievement should pop at this time.
> 
> Good luck!

> **Gelgumi**: After some try and error trying to figure out what was the issue, here are my tips:
> 
> -Enter the dungeon fresh
> -Walk into the boss and do not use any items that isn't the bottle or bow in your way to the boss
> (I used the hook to kill one of the skull spiders along the way and that cancels the cheevo)
> -Once in the boss, just do the challenge as normal, when you get the achievement, it is safe to use your sword for the last hit and kill the boss.

> **CasperRaine**: One of the most fun achievements, not just of this set, but of any set i've played in my short time being on this site. It's fun, it's silly, and it has a slight challenge to it. Perfect achievement for what I'm hoping to get out of this site.
> 
> Follow Gelgumi's advice here. Don't risk using the hook/longshot to kill the Skulltula in the first hallway, kill it with the bow.
> 
> My tip for anyone having trouble with the timing of the bottle swings; Swing a LITTLE earlier than you think you need to. The bottle has a long startup time, but the "swing" stays active for longer than you think. After getting hit by the lighting ball 5 times without my bottle ever starting the swinging motion, that's when I did what I thought was an overcorrection, but turned out it was near perfect.

> **ToastLover**: Imo, this achievement has quite a  bit of flaws that's holding it back from greatness and I personally think needs to be addressed. First is the description, when the average person reads use they'd interpret it as "Don't damage Gannon using anything other than your bows and a bottle" instead the achievement is coded in such a way where if you do so much as mis press your sword button or switch to your hookshot the achievement becomes null and you'd have to start all over again. The second issue is that there's no challenge indicator, how are we suppose to know when it's safe to hit Gannon with our sword or if we even met the requirements if there's no indicator for such. The absence of an indicator becomes even a bigger problem since apparently you can't take out your sword once according to the comments. Many people such as myself go into these challenges without checking the comments first but now that I don't have the achievement because I pressed my B button once on accident I check the comments to find that the reason I didn't get my achievement and have to start a brand new save file in order to get it is because I didn't check the comments before starting the challenge. That sounds absolutely silly and to think that such a mess could've been avoided if there was an indicator. Despite not matching with the description I would've came to the conclusion by myself without having to leave my emulator that I can't take out or use any items besides my bow and bottle. The problem could be solve if you go either way or even both, but that's just my 2 cents, food for thought yk?

> **warski**: Alright, here is how to do it and make sure it works. Make sure you are loaded with arrows - 30 - and have your health full to not get frustrated. Leave the temple and go back in. Do not open any items besides the bow or bottle. Do not even switch to the hookshot. When aiming with the arrow press Z to stop aiming and don't accidently switch to the hookshot. Helps if you have two empty bottles equipped to C and the Bow to not get mixed up. The battle will NOT have the little achievement square on the bottom of the screen. During the battle, use the arrow to use the bow to hit the real Gannon in the painting by listening to the horse gallops. When he is out of the painting - use the bottle to bounce the energy orb to hit him and use the arrows to hit him when he is stunned BUT do not waste arrows. Once you hit the achievement just use the sword to finish him.

> **PauseNaJanta**: Okay, the comments seem confusing and misinterpret what you really need to do. I'll explain it more simply. Well, starting off, if you happen to be at the end of the level, I suggest saving and restarting the game during the boss challenge to have a better chance. Make sure your arrows are full (30 or 25 is enough) and have the vial in your inventory. After restarting the boss challenge and going to the temple, kill the spider with an arrow, proceed to the boss, pass the first phase of the paintings, stay as far away from the boss as possible and wait for him to shoot his spells and bounce them off him, always with a delay after he bounces back. As soon as he falls, shoot four arrows. Do this until the achievement appears, then finish him off with the sword after he falls again.

> **MattKimura**: Here's my tips: Don't use the hookshot in phase 1 or it invalidates the achievement, it's bow only. 
> If you want to use your arrows efficiently in phase 2 without wasting them, here's how the fight works:
> - Phantom Ganon only allows one shot if you down him while he's flying low
> - Phantom Ganon allows up to five shots if you down him when he flies higher and has a proper volley with you
> 
> The bottle actually makes the fight easier because the bottle has a bigger hitbox than the sword and it has a quicker recovery to swing again. Just stand back to give distance so you can time your swings properly. Sometimes the ropes get in the way of the view if you stand too far back. You get used to the timings the more you practice, the three heart challenge should be no challenge after this.

> 
> By far the worst part of this Mastery so far.

> **Vowwels**: Alright, here is the best advice I can give to make this as painless as possible:
> 
> - Go to each location where they spawn (pull up a map/guide etc of locations)
> 
> - Position yourself on Epona at that location, in a way that makes the Poe's spawn. 
> 
> - As long as you don't move, the Poe's will continue respawning and following the same path before disappearing
> 
> - Line up your shot, and time it in a way that you can get 2 shots off before they vanish. 
> 
> This takes some practice, but you'll quickly realize that you cannot just shoot-shoot, as the Poe's have a quick invincibility period while flashing red. Its more of a shoot, 1second, then shoot again
> 
> Hope this helps! This drove me nuts until I realized their pathing habits. 
> 
> Also, yes, Epona's big head WILL DEFINITELY be in your way xD

> **Vowwels**: This is the best map I found, with nice descriptions of the locations as well, due to the tricky nature of positioning on Epona/map arrow-icon being tough to position at times. 
> 
> https://www.thonky.com/ocarina-of-time/big-poe-locations

> **HippocriticalHaz**: Just use the 1/8 heart glitch to get both volvagia achievements at the same time in run.
> 
> Since burn damage, like from fire keese, ticks your health down in 1/16ths if you dont extinguish yourself by rolling, you can intentionally take fire tick damage and just before the moment Link would die to burn, put yourself out to be at 1/16 health.
> 
> Due to some kinda oversight in the game logic, when the timer for heat or drowning looks at your remaining health to decide how long you have to live, it rounds down to the nearest number it can use, which at 1/16th, is 0. So the since its nothing in the games eye, the timer never starts. 
> 
> Now go do the fight in Green Tunic and don't get hit while only using the Hammer.

> **carefreebomb**: Just wanted to confirm that Nayru's Love did work with this for me. I was really curious, so I tested it for any future comment divers that might have trouble with this one (or if it's not intended, to be added to the logic checks).
> 
> - Got Epona to cross the Gerudo bridge
> - After Forest Temple, went back to get the Lens of Truth from the Well as a kid
> - Finished Gerudo Fortress for the gate pass
> - Backwards walked across the river of sand without Longshot or Hover Boots (thanks Youtube)
> - Follow ghost with lens to Fairy Fountain (may as well grab Requiem of Spirit while here too)

> **EnzitoDelGrauzito456**: This achievement is quite difficult, and to complete it you'll need a strategy. I used this one:
> 
> 1 - Have 3 or 4 pots and buy magic potions.
> 
> 2 - Equip the green tunic ONLY when you are extremely close to the Volvagia platform. Every second counts. 
> 
> 3 - use the Jumping Attack with the Megaton Hammer on all hits. If you hit them all, Volvagia will die in 4 hits 
> 
> 4 - when Volvagia is flying, use Din's Fire until he comes down. Din's Fire freezes its timer and Volvagia continues moving. 
> 
> 5 - only use Magic Potions when your magic bar is completely depleted, don't think about saving them, spend them as soon as the Din's Fire limit reaches. 
> 
> This is a very difficult achievement, but with a few tries you will achieve it, good luck!

> **FreudianLisp**: -In phase 2, attack him directly instead of the jellyfish.
> -Second jellyfish was phase, paralyze him and throw 1 nut to hit all of them at once.

> 
> Super easy achievement, got it in first attempt, but I’ve played a lot of rando and fought barinade like 50 times before lol.

> **MINI91**: Here is the easiest way to get it. 
> Equip ten Deku Sticks, Din's Fire and the Boomerang. When the fight starts, circulate around Barinade while Z-targeting the three tentacles holding him up and cut them with the Boomerang. When the last tentacle is cut, hold down the C-button for the Boomerang until the camera gets behind Link. Once it does, release the Boomerang and once Barinade is stunned, Z-target him again, equip a Deku Stick and hit him with the jump-in attack on the A-button (there is a timing to the equipping of the sticks, so get used to that). Equip a new Deku Stick and repeat the jump-in five times (optimally). If you only hit him four times, stun him again with the Boomerang when the jelly fish starts to circulate and hit him the fifth time. Now, let Barinade rotate with the jelly fish around him in the room and avoid contact. You can throw the Boomerang as you please, but it won't hit until about 20 seconds later or so.
> When you hit him with the Boomerang again, and the jelly fish lies on the ground, get in close to Barinade and use Din's Fire to clear the jelly fish. Now, Z-target him and throw the Boomerang at him and when it hits, equip another Deku Stick and use the jump-in attack on the A-button. Barinade will now dig into the ground and shoot electrical shock waves at you. To skip this cycle, run or roll into him intentionally. This will force him out of the ground and you can throw the Boomerang at him again. When it hits, Z-target him and use another Deku Stick to jump-in attack him. He will go underground again, so roll into him again, Z-target him and throw the Boomerang at him again, equip a Deku Stick and use the jump-in attack one final time and you got him. If done with minimal time waste, the achievement should unlock.

> **MolanTpk**: I got beaten like a dog using deku nuts, so I got it on the first try using din fire!

> **Canor**: Getting hit by Barinade in the final phase forces it out of the ground immediately and speeds up the fight a lot. You don't even have to take damage. Just time a roll correctly because lol invincibility frames.

> **Ladybunne**: As others have said, it's really not that bad. I had a lot of fun with this, mostly because playing enough OoT rando makes you good at killing bosses quickly.
> 
> Here's a loose outline for those who need it:
> 
> Phase 1: Quickly boomerang the three top tendrils with Z-Targeting, moving around Barinade as you're waiting for your boomerang to come back. You can double-tap and hold Z to first centre your camera, then target the tendril - I sometimes find that helpful.
> 
> Phase 2: Do a targetless boomerang throw (no Z-Target) at center mass, and if timed correctly it'll detach the jellyfish quickly. Use Deku Nuts to kill them all in one hit. Don't use Din's, nuts are way faster.
> 
> Phase 3: Do either a targeted or a targetless boomerang throw at center mass again (I do targeted) - if you time it very well, it'll connect before the jellyfish hitboxes become active and disconnect them similar to P2. Again, just use one or two Deku Nuts to kill them all. You have plenty of time even if you mess this phase up, so don't fret, just do your best.
> 
> Phase 4: Chase it and hit it with a targeted boomerang throw, jump-slash with a Deku Stick since it's faster than crouch-stabbing (because of the distance), then once it buries into the ground, just roll into it and take the damage / knockback, it's minimal and forces it to get back up much faster than waiting. Do this twice more to end the fight.
> 
> Good luck, it's very doable.

> **Astronis**: Thank you, Altomar. ^^

> **SirBunting**: The key is preventing the boss from entering its spinning jellyfish attack. Go into the boss room with boomerang, nuts, and sticks on your C buttons.
> 1) Throw boomerang at each of its 3 tentacles 1-by-1 while circling it. Use Link's yelp sound effect to know when he caught his boomerang to quickly Z-target and throw again.
> 2) After hitting all 3, try to position yourself between the cracks in the jellyfish armor - so when they burst out, they will miss you. (Perhaps you can hit it with boomerang immediately instead but this is what I did.) Throw boomerang at boss, then run around throwing deku nuts at the scattered jellyfish. You can re-stun the boss after popping 3 or 4 of them. The challenging moment is right after popping the last jellyfish.
> 3) Turn and manually throw the boomerang at the boss as it rises out of the floor. This time, the jellyfish will all be bunched up under him. Kill them with one deku nut. 
> 4) Chase boss and hit him with boomerang, then take out a stick and do a jump attack. This destroys your stick so you can immediately roll into the boss to dislodge it from the floor.
> 5) Repeat 2 more times

I don't have any advice for the actual ping pong section though. Just keep at it and eventually you'll find your rhythm.

> **FreudianLisp**: Protip: before entering the fishing pond on the correct cycle, swim down to the water temple and save inside. Then you can just reset if you mess it up and it’s nice and close to try again.
> 
> Other than that it behaves like any other fish so get it up as close to you as possible before hooking it.

> **SirBunting**: I did it! Allowing the 2 nearby fish to bite, then allowing them to escape without setting the hook, causes them to leave the area. Then you can try the hyrule loach. If it doesn't bite and is close to link, just stay still without pulling your hook out of the water and it will return to its starting position instead of getting scared

> **MINI91**: Here is the easiest way to catch it, even without the sinken lure.
> You will need 80 rupees at least.
> Talk to the pond owner and say yes to fishing, then talk to him again to quit. Repeat this three times in a row. Now, leave the pond area after you quit fishing for the third time and enter the pond area again immediately after. Now the Hyrule Loach has spawned below the lily pads on the left side. Talk to the pond owner for the fourth time and say yes to fishing again.
> Now, bait the two regular fish that swims below the lily pads among the loach to bite and when they do, ignore them. Don't push or move anything on your controller. This will make them release the lure and then they will swim towards the middle of the pond. Now the loach won't be scared away by the other two fish if they bit and you tried to reel them in. The loach is not much of a biter, and it puts up a decent fight. If it lets your lure go and swims towards the middle of the pond, you won't be able to just Z-target it anymore and you will need the sinken lure to catch it, or simply just reset the game and try again. You can save inside the Water Temple to avoid playing the Serenade of Water to warp to Lake Hylia when resetting the game. After you catch the loach, talk to the owner one final time and the achievement will unlock.
>

> **FreudianLisp**: This is the code the game uses to spawn the loach (per the decomp) which is called the moment you walk through the door and load the fishing hole:
> 
> if ((KREG(1) == 1) || ((D_80B7E07D & 3) == 3)) {
>             if (sLinkAge != LINK_AGE_CHILD) {
>                 fishCount = 16;
>             } else {
>                 fishCount = 17;
>             }
>         } else {
>             fishCount = 15;
>         }
> 
> It is not random. There is a bit in memory that tracks how many times you have paid for fishing. If that number is divisible by 3, then the number of fish that spawn in the pool is 17 for child & 16 for adult (Fish ID 16 & 17 are both loach. Yes, two loaches will spawn as child). Note that it spawns immediately upon entering the area after paying 3 times. However, after loading the area you need to pay a 4th time to actually be able to catch the fish, which is why people say you need to pay 4 times to make it spawn when it's technically 3.
> 
> That bit is tracked from the very beginning of the save file, it does not go back to zero when you reset the game. Also, the same bit is incremented for Child & Adult and does not reset when changing ages. So it is easy to get "off-cycle" if you forget how many times you have gone fishing earlier in the save.

> **FreudianLisp**: ^ Note the counter only increments when you pay for fishing, not when entering the area. So if you enter the fishing pond, don't pay, go check on the fish and see the Hylian Loach, you can then leave and enter the area as many times as you want without paying and the loach will still be there every single time.
> 
> With that in mind, if you don't remember where you are in the cycle, follow this strategy to save the headache:
> - Enter the pond and immediately check the fish
> - If you don't see the loach, pay, and then leave. Repeat until you find it.
> - Once you find it, DON'T PAY, just immediately leave the fishing hole.
> - Go save the game inside the Water Temple (the closest respawn point)
> - Head back to the fishing hole and try to catch the loach
> - If you fail & it gets off the hook, reset the game. You will respawn in the Water Temple and the loach will be back the first time you enter. Repeat until you catch it.

> **Chizuru94**: Well, getting it to spawn, despite exact video guides, is the issue for me now. I'm fine with anything else and it not being in the middle of the pond after being scared away, ofc, but ... yeah. Getting it to spawn despite all the advice (and I CHECKED) is frustratingly hard.

> **Chizuru94**: FINALLY got this checked of as well! <3
> 
> So, like others have said already:
> 
> 1. Go to the fishing pond at Lake Hylia
> 2. Buy for a fishing round and quit, buy and quit, buy and quit
> 3. Leave the pond area back to Lake Hylia and SAVE
> 4. Re-enter the pond area (DON'T save anymore now and RELOAD if smth went wrong with the catching attempt) and pay a 4th time
> 
> 5. Go to the left side of the lake and notice the grass sticking out a bit further into the pond, go a little bit into the pond and use Z-target to try and see if the Loach is there
> 
> 6. Wiggle your lure a few times till a fish bites (it's usually not the loach first, so careful and let the first 2 bites escape by having them bite from the wiggling and then not doing ANYTHING) and do this until only the loach is in that area
> 
> 7. Z-target again and try to get the loach to bite by wiggling as much as you can (no reeling in) until it bites
> 
> 8. WHEN it finally bites, immediately press the button for reeling in and hold it till you did so, meanwhile, tilt your analog stick in the direction the fish's head is looking right now and adjust it every time, stop the analog stick movements while it jumps and also go a bit more easy on it/no moving when it produces these dust clouds underwater.
> 
> 9. Keep doing the above and reload the save if smth goes wrong and you'll get it in about 2 hours or less, hopefully! <3
> 
> And credit goes to a few YT comments and tips on here, ofc. 
> 
> Big thank you, guys! I might just get this mastery after all, I hope! :)
> 
> And this will be my biggest hardcore mastery up till now then. Hope it works out for everyone!

**Description:** Defeat Bongo Bongo without using the Lens of Truth and without healing with 3 hearts only

> **Meikitamemo**: Stun Bongo Bongo's hands and shoot in the middle when he charges! cannot use any kind of healing during the battle [Health drops included]

> **Server**: BenGhazi edited this achievement.

> **Server**: DrPixel edited this achievement.

> **Server**: SporyTike edited this achievement.

> **WolfferroW**: Easy Strategy: Have 40 or more arrows, before the boss's room use Favore's Wind(FW) and save, if you fail, reset the game and use FW to teleport near the boss's room, don't forget that you can't use a lens of truth on the boss.

> **WolfferroW**: In battle, try to hit one hand without Hover Boots and when the other hand gets close, try to get out of her reach and then hit. At the end aim in the middle of them for the boss to appear and finish him off. Biggoron's sword is a great help.

> **Server**: DanielARP edited this achievement.

> **Server**: ZZKer edited this achievement.

> **ZZKer**: fixed a typo

> **Baifomet**: Not that complicated, guys. When it comes to charge you, just spam sword spinning attack and it will be down easily.

> **PixelRoast**: As soon as the intro cutscene ends, Z-target a hand and take a few seconds to back away until both hands are visible on your screen, drumming. Shoot the Z-targeted hand with an arrow and immediately switch Z-target to the other hand.
> 
> If the 2nd hand (hopefully) prepares either a sideways slap, sideways chop, or downward slap, you can just fire an arrow at it. But if the 2nd hand prepares a fist, dodge it (I like to not break Z-target here), then as it regroups for it's next attack, fire the arrow.
> 
> Now center your view as the hands line up to charge you and free aim your bow to shoot the invisible eye, or charge and time a spin attack. Get a feel for how late you can risk the stun. I usually fired two shots at the eye to be safe in case the first was a little off. Stunning the boss near to you rewards you with a chance to get in two jump attacks or about four regular hits before repeating the cycle but timing is tight.
> 
> A master sword does 2 damage and a jump attack does 4 damage. Bongo Bongo has 32 HP, so unless you're getting in 8 damage per cycle, then you should expect five or more cycles. In shooting the hands, you'll probably have a few misses and if you're also shooting the charging eye, 4-5 rounds means you can easily burn through 20 arrows. Be careful with spamming arrows and running out, the hands drop magic or arrow loot when you shoot them. Try to quickly roll to the ammo if you're low and your bow isn't upgraded.

> **sandroRetro**: I thought about farming biggoron sword because that guy always gave me trouble as a kid.
> 
> Turns out it is extremely easy.
> 
> Shoot both hands.
> 
> Charge a jump slash. 
> When the first are almost at you release it.
> hit the shit out of bongo bongo with the sword.
> 
> Took me 3 of these cycle and done.

> **MasterSwordUltima**: Shoot one hand, back up, shoot the other, then keep rotating the analog stick and B for spin attacks. I used Biggoron's Sword and by luck timed it to hit enough times to get a 1 cycle. Pretty cool way to end this temple.

> **sandroRetro**: I wrote Charge a jump lash above but I mean charge the spin.

> **MoozipanCheese**: My tips:
> 
> As mentioned above, use Farore's Wind on the room before the boss and then save the game, so if you fail you can quickly reset and warp back by using Farore's Wind again.
> 
> Make sure the Lens of Truth isn't in your c-button inventory slots; the Achievement didn't unlock for me the first time I succeeded and I think this is why, even though I didn't actually use it.
> 
> Before jumping down to activate the boss, equip the Biggoron's Sword and do a jump attack. During the battle, use arrows on both his hands and shoot in between his hands after they pass you (don't aim up or down, just centre) to stun Bongo Bongo when he's closest to you. Now, the trick is to run up to him, crouch (don't lock on) and repeatedly stab; this is known as Power Crouch Stabbing and each stab will have the same damage as a jump attack. Doing this should allow you to two-cycle him.
> 
> Took me a couple of attempts to get the 'feel' of the boss, but he's not so bad once you figure him out. At just three hearts, most of his attacks will kill you quickly. His grab attack will one-hit you.

> **Server**: Altomar edited this achievement's description.

> **GalaxiusYT**: It's much easier take out the left hand first (on Link's right side) so you don't get bounced around as much or fire at the wrong time and miss. I was dreading this one, but it's pretty easy to z-target + shoot both hands, fire an arrow straight ahead as he charges, and then jump attack 5 times with the Biggoron's sword.

> **Saive10**: This was a genuine nightmare before i read the comments here. Getting Favore's Wind and 40 arrows just saved a lot of pain of constantly coming back to the boss room.

> **PauseNaJanta**: A tip for you to return to the Boss if you happen to fail the achievement! I always recommend that you make a portal before the Boss room in the room with invisible platforms. If you happen to die, don't continue. Close the emulator and quickly return. Use a turbo key on your preferred controller, return to the portal and do the challenge again. You need to do this because the path to the Boss is long, and if you die in this part, the game will save manually at the beginning.

> **ReadingTheGame**: Espanol: Este desafo puede ser logrado si tienes los 20 corazones. Mi recomendacion es que dejes a Ganon te haga dao hasta que tengas dos corazones. Luego golpeate con la pared de fuego y as reduces el dano para que te quede preciso a 1 corazon.

> **RDPateau**: i think the easiest way to do this is in a 3 heart run with the defence enhancer, ganos hit takes exactly 2 hearts off so just take one hit and finish the battle

> **Rampagingtaru**: If you do this in the 3 heart run. Before you reach the outside with zelda in the steps just keep running into the burning rocks till you have one heart left. Then you can get both done in one go.

> **zzzzzzz**: Get to an odd number of hearts, then let Ganon widdle you down to one heart. If need be, touch the ring of fire on the outer boundary to aid in getting one heart or less. If you're not confident in not getting hit by Ganon once you have one heart or less, use Nayru's love.

> **MINI91**: Fight against Ganon as you normally would until he gets knocked down for the first time and Ganon and you get the Master Sword back. Now, you can continue to hit his tail three more times first, or you can let him hit you until you get close to one heart. When you know you can't take more hits from Ganon, walk into the ring of fire around the arena until you get Link down to one heart.
> Now, use Nayru's Love to protect you. 
> Getting the "Magic Gauge increase" upgrade, is recommended for more protection and more attempts, but not required.
> Don't Z-target Ganon when you get to the second phase at first, just roll between his legs, and then Z-target his tail, or simply just swing at it without Z-targeting. You need the Master Sword for the second phase to finish it and to deal the final blow when you knock him down for the second time. If you see Nayru's Love startting to flash rapidly, just spam the mapped C-button to activated it again as soon as possible, so you won't waste as much time as I did before figuring this out.

> **TrashPaddy**: Nayru's Love tip was key here

> **alvayuso**: After retrieving the Master Sword Ganon can take another 5 hits at least, just use Nayru’s Love and keep hitting him until the last blow

> **MateusSodr3**: Flechas de Luz deixar ele paralisado

> **MoozipanCheese**: Great tips here. Just wanted to add that the Achievement doesn't unlock until Zelda and Link have a conversation in the sky (?) after Ganondorf curses your descendents.

> **oroTe**: If you want to be an epic gamer/don't have nayru's love, you can also just run right under his legs and he will never hit you.

> **jamboree**: I failed this first time because I didn't realise this: Biggoron's sword is ineffective for the second phase of the Ganon fight. I hit him a few times with the Biggoron's sword to get him low (I thought!) and then got myself down to 1 heart, activated Nayru's love, and the fight went on for AGES. I swapped back and forth between the master sword and the Biggoron's sword, but Nayru's love expired in the meantime and he hit me.
> 
> In the second phase, ONLY the master sword can do half decent damage. Biggoron's sword looks like it does damage but it only does a tiny bit. You'd have thought they would have the damage animation disabled for anything other than the master sword, but hey...

> **TheHy1ianGuy**: just use bombchus to lower down health did it before doing final blow and it worked

> **JohnnyTheHotGuy**: Are 3 hearts required?

> **Rampagingtaru**: No it shouldn't be.

> **demonlord421**: Every weapon except the Master Sword does 1 damage to Ganon, which is less than the Kokiri Sword and the Broken Giant's Knife do to most enemies.  To add to the comment about rolling between Ganon's legs, there is an efficient way to do it without breaking the target lock.  Hit Ganon in the face with the Hookshot first.  Not only does this put Link's Sword away if it wasn't already so Link can roll while targeting Ganon, it also stops Ganon from acting for a moment.  Not nearly as long as a Light Arrow does obviously, but it stops him for around a second which is surprisingly generous.

Nayrus Love is handy, though I abandoned using it when I realised you can't use Light Arrows at the same time. > **RaffeyGoode**: Honestly I think the best route is drop below health by whatever means (I happened to get hit a few times on the way down, so when Ganon hit me I was able to get to 1/2 heart left). Have green potions, light arrow his face, roll under (you can roll as long as you don't switch weapons) and use the megaton hammer/Big gorons sword until you get the master sword. Rinse and repeat using the Master Sword and you don't need Nayru's love or anything. Stunning with the longshot is too short of a window, and you cannot use light arrows with Nayru's love active. So just stick with light arrows, dodge roll, and strike.

> **ThePixelJunkie**: I might be in the minority but I've always beat him without magic.
> 
> I've always ran up to him, rolled under his legs while he attacked behind me. 1st phase of Ganon, you have plenty of time to turn around and z target tail, then jump strike with Biggoron sword or hammer. (Have to be kinda close with hammer though due to its reach) Run/roll away and quick turn towards him. Rinse and repeat.
> 
> 2nd phase when you get master sword back, he's quicker but same strategy works more or less. A safer strategy for 2nd phase while using this method would be to roll under legs, slash with master sword (no z target), roll/run forward a bit and quick turn. Rinse and repeat.
> 
> He'll be closer when he chases you, compared to 1st phase after rolling away, but it's manageable. Not Z targeting also gives enough time to avoid his 2nd slash, if he does decide to do one.
> 
> https://www.twitch.tv/videos/1822873946
> 
> Probably not the safest nor fastest way to do it, but it worked for me.

> **RetroPestro**: I agree @thepixeljunkie, I've always done it rolling between the legs. Nintendo Official Magazine used to promote this trick back in the 90s and it's great tactic imo

> **GreatBeardicus**: Wait, why is this limited to a 3-heart run if you're supposed to not get hit at all. This is just as difficult in a 20-heart run as it is in a 3-heart run, isn't it?

> **ColonelKurtz**: Thanks for the video @ThePixelJunkie

> Got it 2nd try after watching your video.

> **TheHy1ianGuy**: did the 3 heart run with 1 bottle

> **architextured**: Personally, I was struggling a little bit during this fight and I found this to be the easiest method:
> 
> For the 1st half (when you don't have the Master Sword), equip Biggoron's Sword (or Megaton Hammer if you don't have that), back away from Ganon, and as he walks towards you roll underneath him, then Z-target and jump-attack his tail.  Then back up a little bit again and repeat.
> 
> For the 2nd half, keep your Master Sword out, and don't Z-target at all.  I found it hard to have the time to jump-attack since he's quicker in this half, so instead I rolled underneath him like before but then did an immediate quick-spin out of my roll (rotate the stick and press attack).  This allowed me to both damage/stun him, and have the time to easily back away so I didn't risk taking any damage.

> **Jaybeegee**: Personally did not find the boss fight hard at all, struggled more with escaping the castle with 3 hearts lol

> **BigWooper**: Just want to mention here that Nayru's Love can make this challenge super easy. You can get hit while Nayru's Love is up and still obtain the achievement. I think it would be more accurate if the description said "without taking damage" instead of "without getting hit", but it's nothing to make a big deal out of.

> **Rebourne07**: Thank you @Olra! I was about to open a ticket for this, but your comment saved me. For anyone in my position...I was missing one compass (Fire Temple) when I first beat the game. > - Inside Jabu-Jabu's Belly (2) (Compass, Map)
> - Forest Temple (3) (Compass, Map, Boss Key)
> - Fire Temple (3) (Compass, Map, Boss Key)
> - Ice Cavern (2) (Compass, Map)
> - Water Temple (3) (Compass, Map, Boss Key)
> - Bottom of the Well (2) (Compass, Map)
> - Shadow Temple (3) (Compass, Map, Boss Key)
> - Spirit Temple (1) (Map) "Child Link"
> - Spirit Temple (2) (Compass, Boss Key) "Adult Link"
> - Ganon's Castle (1) (Boss Key)

> **andygood**: Heads up, "Complete the game with every Map, Compass, and Boss Key from all dungeons" could be a little clearer, you specifically have to collect every Map, Compass, and Boss Key /then/ complete the game.
> 
> If you went back to get something you missed after beating the game, you'll have to return to beat Ganondorf and Ganon again for the achievement to trigger.

> **kaitri**: im at 26/26 so i think i'll get this as soon as ganon is deadge, but why is this missable?

> **FajitaQueen**: @kaitri it's marked as missable because if you complete Jabu Jabu without getting it (which is mandatory for the Blind Explorer achievement), then upon reentry later Ruto isn't present so you can't put her on the switch to open the door to the room with the Map.
> 
> You can still get it with a fairly easily performed glitch to trigger the switch, but even so that does make it missable per the site guidelines.

> **FajitaQueen**: complete Jabu Jabu without getting the map*, that is.
